# List of commercial nuclear reactors

**URL**: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_commercial_nuclear_reactors
**Collected**: 2026-06-15 15:23:52
**Categories**: Nuclear_reactors, Nuclear_power_stations, Lists_of_buildings_and_structures, Nuclear_technology-related_lists, Technology-related_lists_by_country

---

This article is about commercial nuclear fission reactors. For other nuclear reactor lists, see [List of nuclear reactors](/wiki/List_of_nuclear_reactors "List of nuclear reactors").

This is a [dynamic list](/wiki/Wikipedia:WikiProject_Lists#Dynamic_lists "Wikipedia:WikiProject Lists") and may never be able to satisfy particular standards for completeness. You can help by [editing the page](/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_commercial_nuclear_reactors "Special:EditPage/List of commercial nuclear reactors") to add missing items, with references to [reliable sources](/wiki/Wikipedia:Reliable_sources "Wikipedia:Reliable sources").

This is a list of all the commercial [nuclear reactors](/wiki/Nuclear_reactor "Nuclear reactor") in the world, sorted by country, with operational status. The list only includes civilian [nuclear power](/wiki/Nuclear_power "Nuclear power") reactors used to generate electricity for a [power grid](/wiki/Power_grid "Power grid"). All commercial nuclear reactors use [nuclear fission](/wiki/Nuclear_fission "Nuclear fission"). As of May 2025, there are 439 operable power reactors in the world, with a combined electrical capacity of 397.7 GW. Additionally, there are 68 reactors under construction and 108 reactors planned, with a combined capacity of 74 GW and 103 GW, respectively, while 359 more reactors are proposed.[](#cite_note-WNA-1) For non-power reactors, see [List of nuclear research reactors](/wiki/List_of_nuclear_research_reactors "List of nuclear research reactors"). For fuel plants see [List of nuclear reprocessing plants](/wiki/List_of_nuclear_reprocessing_plants "List of nuclear reprocessing plants"). Where not otherwise specified, all information is sourced from the Power Reactor Information System (PRIS) of the [International Atomic Energy Agency](/wiki/International_Atomic_Energy_Agency "International Atomic Energy Agency") (IAEA).[](#cite_note-:3-2)

In the following tables, the _capacity_ (expressed in [megawatt](/wiki/Megawatt "Megawatt"), MW) refers to the _net capacity_ , or the maximum electricity output under reference ambient conditions, after deducting the losses within the system including the energy transformers.[](#cite_note-pris_Glossary-3)
